We study this train-sparse / infer-sparse setting by learning a pose-aware diffusion prior from one 2D projection per training sample, then guiding diffusion sampling with measured-view fidelity, shared-volume reprojection, and projection-physics consistency for tomographic reconstruction. On two public CT datasets across 1-, 4-, and 8-view settings, the proposed method achieves the best novel-view synthesis scores and best or competitive reconstruction quality compared with baselines that use dense-volume supervision.

</details>


### [35] [SSQA: Sibling-Selective Quadtree Attention for Hierarchical Modeling in Perception Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38320)
*Yufan Chen,Arnav Bali,Angela Liu,Laura Zheng,Ming C. Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Sibling-Selective Quadtree Attention (SSQA)，以结构化全四叉树分层建模token，实现线性时间复杂度的注意力替代方案，在机器人与自动驾驶感知任务上以更低算力取得不劣于甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航（无人机、自动驾驶）的感知数据具有显著轴对齐结构先验（天空在上、地形在下、中间为障碍物；道路布局、车道线、周围agent），比通用图像任务更结构化。标准注意力为二次复杂度，效率低，故希望利用该结构先验提升效率与效果。

Method: 将图像token按结构化、完整的四叉树进行分层建模，提出Sibling-Selective Quadtree Attention，在四叉树的兄弟节点间进行选择性注意力计算；并给出解析复杂度分析以证明线性时间特性，同时与Mamba 2、Quadtree Attention等方法进行推理速度实测对比。

Result: 复杂度分析保证线性时间的特征建模；多任务基准实验表明，SSQA在仅需极小部分计算成本的情况下，性能至少与其他方法持平，甚至明显更优。

Conclusion: 利用轴对齐结构先验的四叉树式分层注意力可作为vanilla注意力（二次复杂度）的高效线性替代，在机器人与自动驾驶感知任务中同时兼具效率与精度优势。

Abstract: Perception tasks for navigation in robotics, including aerial platforms such as drones and autonomous driving systems, are inherently structured. Drone-mounted cameras typically capture sky above, terrain below, and obstacles or man-made structures in between, while driving data often contains organized road layouts, lane markings, and surrounding agents. Motivated by these axis-aligned structural priors, we note that such information is typically more structured than in generic image tasks. We hypothesize that processing information in a quadtree-esque manner can not only model features effectively in a hierarchical manner, but also offers an efficient linear-time alternative to vanilla attention mechanisms, which run in quadratic time. In this paper, we propose Sibling-Selective Quadtree Attention (SSQA), which models image tokens hierarchically as a structured, full quadtree. We show analytical complexity analysis that guarantees linear-time feature modeling, in addition to empirical experiments comparing inference speeds with other popular modeling approaches, such as Mamba 2 and Quadtree Attention. Our results, benchmarked across several tasks, show that we achieve results at least as good, if not notably better, as others at a fraction of the computational costs.

</details>


### [36] [Knowledge-structured and evidence-aware semantic self-distillation for glomerular pathology foundation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38346)
*Qiming He,Jing Li,Zihan Wang,Tian Guan,Yifei Ma,Zimo Zhao,Yanxia Wang,Hongjing Chen,Yexing Zhang,Yizhi Wang,Xinrui Chen,Xitong Ling,Minxi Ouyang,Yujie Xie,Lianghui Zhu,Yiqing Liu,Qingxia Hou,Shuyan Zhao,Xiaoqin Wang,Lili Ma,Qiang Huang,Zhiyuan Shen,Junru Cheng,Siqi Zeng,Chao He,Yonghong He,Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GloSAGE：利用预训练视觉教师模型预测的病变图谱构建结构化病理描述，实现标注高效的语义自蒸馏视觉-语言预训练框架，扩展肾小球视觉基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型虽学到丰富病理形态，但缺乏显式的概念级监督；视觉-语言预训练可提供此类监督，却依赖昂贵的图文配对数据。

Method: 语义自蒸馏框架：以预训练视觉教师输出的病变图谱构造结构化病理描述；采用可靠性引导选择确定图文锚点；以知识结构化病理文本合成（Knowledge-Structured Pathology Text Synthesis）和证据引导负样本构建（Evidence-Guided Negative-pair Construction）处理部分可观察病变；图文对齐仅训练投影层、冻结视觉编码器；引入中间语义迁移层（Semantic Transfer Layer）为下游任务供特征。

Result: 在GloMillion上开发，于7个肾小球级与病例级队列评估：支持零样本病变识别、图文检索、难负样本判别与概念干预分析；多数病变识别任务优于源视觉模型并改善病变分级；病例级疾病分类、损伤分级及基线肾功能估计具竞争力；消融验证知识结构化文本与证据引导负样本的联合贡献，中间迁移层较完整投影输出提升平均病变识别ROC-AUC；以GloSAGE特征为条件的冻结语言模型可生成肾小球描述。

Conclusion: 教师模型导出的病变图谱可为肾小球视觉基础模型提供标注高效的语义监督，有效扩展其概念级能力。

Abstract: Visual foundation models learn rich pathological morphology but lack explicit concept-level supervision. Vision--language pretraining can provide this supervision but requires costly image--text pairs. Here, we introduce GloSAGE, a semantic self-distillation framework that uses lesion profiles predicted by a pretrained visual teacher to construct structured pathology descriptions. Reliability-guided selection identifies image--text anchors, while Knowledge-Structured Pathology Text Synthesis and Evidence-Guided Negative-pair Construction account for partially observed lesions. Image--text alignment trains projection layers while retaining the frozen visual encoder; an intermediate Semantic Transfer Layer supplies features for downstream tasks. Developed on GloMillion and evaluated across seven glomerulus- and case-level cohorts, GloSAGE supported zero-shot lesion recognition, image--text retrieval, hard-negative discrimination and concept-intervention analyses. It outperformed its source visual model in most lesion-recognition tasks and improved lesion grading, while supporting competitive case-level disease classification, injury grading and baseline renal function estimation. Ablations supported the combined contributions of knowledge-structured text and evidence-guided negatives, and the intermediate transfer layer improved mean lesion-recognition ROC-AUC relative to the complete projection output. A frozen language model conditioned on GloSAGE features generated glomerular descriptions. These findings suggest that teacher-derived lesion profiles can provide annotation-efficient semantic supervision for extending a glomerular visual foundation model.

</details>


### [37] [Aligning Misaligned Benchmarks for Single-View Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38349)
*Yang-Che Tseng,Erqun Dong,Jeong-gi Kwak,Gopal Sharma,Shakiba Kheradmand,Stephan Garbin,Hossam Isack,Kwang Moo Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出单视图新视角合成评测中的关键问题在于参考帧与GT位姿的对齐，提出用传统2D-2D/2D-3D图像匹配对齐3D重建，并仅在共视区域评估；结果显示对齐后显式3D高斯方法达到SOTA，并给出基于视频生成器与几何估计器的简单强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视图新视角合成方法进展迅速，但性能量化评估尚未标准化。作者认为一个重要因素在于各方法自身参考帧如何与所谓GT位姿对齐：现有方法常在测试时优化目标位姿来完成对齐，而相机位姿与渲染精度的优化景观复杂，该问题本质不适定，导致方法被在任意局部最优处评估；另一些方法则依赖用训练与测试视图重建整个场景的3D结构来对齐位姿，可能不可行。因此需要一个更可靠、可行的评测协议。

Method: 提出使用传统2D-to-2D与2D-to-3D图像匹配技术来对齐3D重建（而非测试时优化位姿或全场景重建）。同时，只在不同视图之间的共视区域内进行评估，因为只有在该区域才能定义“正确”的GT。此外给出一个简单基线：直接组合现成视频生成器与前馈几何估计器，无需任何任务特定训练。

Result: 在正确坐标对齐下，使用显式3D高斯的新视角合成方法达到SOTA，尽管文献中此前报道其被纯图像2D方法超越。该简单基线（现成视频生成器+前馈几何估计器）已可与专门为新视角合成训练的SOTA方法相比，且无需任务特定训练。

Conclusion: 评测中的坐标对齐与评估区域界定对结论影响重大；基于图像匹配的3D对齐+共视区评估是更合理可行的协议；据此显式3D高斯方法实为SOTA，且通用视频生成与几何估计的组合已构成强基线，提示需重新审视既有评测结论。

Abstract: While single-view novel view synthesis methods have been dramatically enhanced recently, how to quantify their performance has yet to be standardized. In this work, we demonstrate that an important factor lies in how each method's own reference frame is aligned with the so-called ground-truth pose. Existing methods often rely on test-time optimization of target poses for this alignment, which inherently is ill-posed due to the complexity of the optimization landscape of camera poses vs render accuracy, leading to methods being evaluated at an arbitrary local optimum. Some recognize this limitation and thus rely on 3D reconstructions of entire scenes using training and test views to align poses, which may be infeasible. In this work, instead, we propose to use traditional 2D-to-2D and 2D-to-3D image matching techniques to align 3D reconstructions. We also evaluate only within co-visible regions, between different views, which is the only place where a `correct' ground truth can be defined. Our evaluations show that with proper coordinate alignment, novel-view synthesis methods that use explicit 3D Gaussians achieve the state of the art, despite being previously reported in the literature to be outperformed by pure image-based 2D methods. We further provide a simple baseline that directly utilizes an off-the-shelf video generator and a feed-forward geometry estimator. Our simple baseline is already comparable to state-of-the-art methods specifically trained for novel-view synthesis, without any task-specific training.

</details>


### [38] [WeVisCut: The Whole Editing Room in One Agent From Narration to Long-Form Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38352)
*Chenyu Mu,Wenming Weng,Qinrui Wang,Xingsong Ye,Hao Yu,Jun He,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Erkun Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeVisCut：将工具执行与策略学习结合的长视频脚本转视频系统，用编辑反演+可验证重放的四阶段数据管线从3,750个成品视频中自动构建13万+训练样本，训练单一9B策略（harness内共享生产状态），并提出100任务基准与5项指标，性能接近Claude Opus 5且每成功输出分钟生成token数少约33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从旁白生成长视频需在镜头间保持连贯的相互依赖决策。现有agentic剪辑多用通用模型驱动的多阶段工作流，紧凑、任务适配策略在质量-成本权衡上的表现尚不明确，本文填补这一空白。

Method: 提出WeVisCut系统：agent harness维护共享生产状态，使工具反馈驱动后续决策与修复；因成品视频不暴露决策过程，设计四阶段数据管线，结合编辑反演与可验证重放，将恢复的剪辑意图转为可执行监督；据此训练单一9B策略在统一harness内协调生产与故障恢复；同时构建100任务基准及5项评估剪辑质量与交付可靠性的指标。

Result: 在共享工具环境下，WeVisCut显著优于其基座模型，达到与Claude Opus 5相当的性能，而每成功输出分钟生成token数减少约33倍；在自建指标上也优于任务匹配的原生剪辑系统。

Conclusion: 紧凑、任务适配的单一策略配合状态共享harness，可在长视频脚本转视频任务上实现高效（token成本大幅降低）且可靠的质量，验证了从成品视频反演监督以训练专用agent策略的可行性。

Abstract: Producing long-form videos from narration requires interdependent decisions that remain coherent across shots. Existing agentic editors often use multi-stage workflows driven by general models, leaving the quality-cost trade-off of compact, task-adapted policies unclear. We address this gap with WeVisCut, a long-form script-to-video system that connects tool execution with policy learning. Its agent harness maintains shared production state so that tool feedback informs subsequent decisions and repairs. Because finished videos do not expose these decisions, our four-stage data pipeline combines editing inversion with verified replay to turn recovered editing intent into executable supervision. Applied to 3,750 finished videos, this pipeline yields over 130K training examples. We use this supervision to train a single 9B policy that coordinates production and recovers from failures within the same harness. Moreover, we introduce a 100-task benchmark and five metrics assessing editing quality and delivery reliability. Under a shared tool environment, WeVisCut substantially outperforms its base model and achieves comparable performance to Claude Opus 5 while using nearly 33x fewer generated tokens per successful output minute. It also outperforms task-matched native editors on our proposed metrics.

</details>


### [39] [Volley3D: Unified 3D Full-Sequence Reconstruction from Monocular Broadcasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38358)
*Noâm Boussouf,Adrien Maglo,Mohamed Chaouch,Hideo Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 面向单目排球赛事转播，提出无需3D训练监督的物理驱动流水线，校正地面投影透视漂移并重建3D球轨迹，显著降低3D空间漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视角体育转播3D重建对自动化分析与沉浸式AR/VR回放至关重要，但移动相机难以标定、深度歧义严重。既有方法多针对网球等隔网击球类运动，依赖平面投影假设；排球具有不可预测的多接触序列与高海拔跳跃，使该假设失效，且缺少3D训练监督。

Method: 构建完整、物理先验驱动的单目排球转播流水线：(1) 校正模块，将时序跳跃检测与牛顿插值耦合，修正地平面投影引起的透视漂移，实现球员3D定位；(2) 球体3D轨迹估计，设计专用子轨迹分段架构与碰撞匹配算法；(3) 将物理方程锚定到重建的3D手部顶点，把含噪的2D球检测提升为时空一致的3D场景。

Result: 在多相机基准上评估，相较2D重投影基线显著降低3D空间漂移。

Conclusion: 无需3D真值监督即可从单目转播中重建时空一致的球员与球体3D场景，验证了物理先验与跳跃/碰撞建模在复杂团队运动中的有效性。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction from single-view sports broadcast is a pivotal capability for automated analytics and immersive AR/VR replays, yet notoriously ill-posed due to difficult-to-calibrate moving cameras and depth ambiguity. Unlike widely studied racket sports, team sports such as volleyball feature unpredictable multi-contact sequences and high altitude jumps. These dynamics categorically invalidate the standard planar projection assumptions relied upon by existing methods. To resolve these limitations without 3D training supervision, we propose a complete, physics-grounded pipeline tailored to monocular broadcast volleyball. First, we include a correction module to the perspective drift induced by ground-plane projection by coupling temporal jump spotting with Newtonian interpolation for 3D player placement. Second, for 3D ball trajectory estimation, we design a dedicated sub-trajectory segmentation architecture alongside a collision-matching algorithm. By anchoring physical equations to reconstructed 3D hand vertices, we lift noisy ball 2D detections into a spatiotemporally coherent 3D scene. Evaluations on multi-camera benchmarks demonstrate our approach significantly reduces 3D spatial drift compared to 2D-reprojection baselines.

</details>


### [40] [SparseTokenGS: Gaussian-based Sparse-View CBCT Reconstruction via Reusable Token Pool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38360)
*Zihan Deng,Zhenzhen Gong,Yujie Liu,Jiaxin Gong,Dingkun Liu,Kexian Xie,Yixing Huang,Peng Jin,Tianye Niu,Zhisheng Wang,Fenglin Liu,Hongming Shan,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SparseTokenGS，用可复用token池为前馈高斯方法在解码阶段保留全扫描上下文，实现稀疏视角锥束CT单次前向重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角锥束CT易产生严重伪影；现有前馈高斯方法仅在构建场时写入全扫描信息，坐标解码只能使用局部特征与邻近锚点，导致扫描内体数据不一致、重建质量显著下降。

Method: 构建可复用的共享token池；通过Anchor Token Fusion在锚点处、Point-Token Attention在查询坐标处查询该池；保留局部高斯先验，一次前向完成重建，无需逐扫描优化或投影域损失。

Result: 在三个公开胸部与牙科数据集上重建性能最优；临床牙科扫描仅用6个投影即达PSNR 29.51 dB、SSIM 0.842（对比全视角参考），现成牙齿-牙髓分割仍可分离结构并支持根管定位；火山岩与鸡肉实验显示同物体自适应后有效。

Conclusion: 可复用token池使前馈重建在解码全程保持扫描级上下文，有望服务快速临床病灶定位与分析。

Abstract: Sparse-view cone-beam computed tomography is widely used for fast, low-dose volumetric imaging and lesion localization in dentistry and image-guided interventions, but it introduces severe artifacts. Feed-forward Gaussian methods transfer the reconstruction cost to training and decode each coordinate from projection-aligned features and neighboring anchors. However, information from the whole scan is written only when the field is constructed and cannot be retrieved during coordinate decoding, leaving the volume inconsistent within the scan. This is a common limitation of such methods: once the field is fixed, decoding uses only local features and reconstruction quality drops substantially. We propose SparseTokenGS, a Gaussian-based method for sparse-view cone-beam computed tomography reconstruction via a reusable token pool. Anchor Token Fusion and Point-Token Attention query this shared pool at the anchors and at the query coordinates, respectively. The local Gaussian prior is retained, and the volume is reconstructed in one forward pass, without per-scan optimization or a projection-domain loss. On three public chest and dental datasets, SparseTokenGS achieves the highest reconstruction performance among the compared methods. On clinical dental scans, reconstruction from only six projections reaches 29.51 decibels in peak signal-to-noise ratio and a structural similarity of 0.842 against the full-view reference, and off-the-shelf tooth-and-pulp segmentation keeps these structures separable for root-canal localization. Experiments on volcanic rock and chicken show performance after same-object adaptation. A reusable token pool gives feed-forward reconstruction a mechanism that keeps scan-wide context available through decoding and can benefit future rapid clinical lesion localization and analysis. The code is available at: https://github.com/FrankDengAI/SparseTokenGS.

</details>


### [41] [Visually Irrelevant Hallucinations in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38389)
*Ke Zou,Nan Zhou,Tao Yang,Meng Liu,Linchao He,Zehui Liao,Ling Huang,Guangren Huang,Yidi Chen,Yi Zhang,Hu Chen,Huazhu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR，一个模型无关的事后框架，在短语级别估计医疗视觉语言模型的"视觉无关幻觉"风险，通过局部反事实扰动与双重视觉支持审计线索实现检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗VLM在放射报告生成与Med-VQA中表现优异，但可能生成缺乏视觉证据或与图像内容矛盾的临床上有意义的陈述（视觉无关幻觉VIH），此类细粒度可靠性失效尚未被系统研究。

Method: CLEAR将生成响应分解为可视觉评估的临床短语；对每个短语进行短语条件定位以识别候选视觉区域，并施加局部反事实干预扰动该区域；用两类互补审计线索刻画干预效果——响应线索（生成响应的语义与临床有意义变化）与对齐线索（图文兼容性变化），二者归一化后联合建模以估计短语级幻觉风险。

Result: 在覆盖RRG与Med-VQA的四个数据集上，CLEAR在多种医疗与通用VLM上优于最先进幻觉检测基线；在MIMIC-CXR上将AUPRC从85.10%提升至90.23%；并在基于GT构建的受控VIH基准上进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: 视觉无关幻觉是医疗VLM重要的细粒度可靠性失效模式，模型无关的视觉支持审计对构建更可信的多模态临床生成具有价值。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) show strong performance in radiology report generation (RRG) and medical visual question answering (Med-VQA), producing free-form clinical descriptions and answers from medical images. However, they may generate clinically meaningful statements that lack visual evidence or contradict image content. We refer to these errors as visually irrelevant hallucinations and investigate them at the phrase level. We propose CLEAR (Counterfactual Localized Evaluation of Alignment and Response), a model-agnostic post-hoc framework for phrase-level hallucination risk estimation. CLEAR decomposes generated responses into visually assessable clinical phrases. For each phrase, phrase-conditioned grounding identifies a candidate visual region, which is selectively perturbed by a localized counterfactual intervention. CLEAR characterizes the intervention using two complementary auditing cues: response cues, capturing semantic and clinically meaningful changes in the generated response, and alignment cues, measuring changes in image-phrase compatibility. These cues are normalized and jointly modeled to estimate phrase-level hallucination risk. Across four datasets spanning RRG and Med-VQA, CLEAR outperforms state-of-the-art hallucination detection baselines across multiple medical and general-purpose VLMs. On MIMIC-CXR, CLEAR improves AUPRC from 85.10% to 90.23%. Its effectiveness is further validated on a GT-derived controlled VIH benchmark. These results establish visually irrelevant hallucination as an important fine-grained reliability failure mode in medical VLMs and demonstrate the value of model-agnostic visual-support auditing for more trustworthy multimodal clinical generation.

</details>


### [42] [Structure-retained low-rank adapters for weather synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38413)
*Shunxin Wang,Alexandros Stergiou,Luuk Spreeuwers,Estefania Talavera,Nicola Strisciuglio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于结构保持LoRA的天气合成框架，利用SDXL注意力块中处理结构信息的定位，实现无需额外外部条件控制的高保真天气转换数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外视觉模型部署受恶劣天气等环境干扰限制，跨天气鲁棒性至关重要；但现有数据集在同一场景观测中混入行人、车辆变化与视角变化等动态因素，难以将纯天气引起的分布偏移与场景本身解耦，亟需高质量、仅含天气效应的数据。

Method: 提出天气合成框架：分析SDXL中的注意力块，定位图像到图像翻译过程中结构信息主要被处理的层；针对这些块学习结构LoRA矩阵（structure-retaining Low-Rank Adapters），并用于天气合成，从而摆脱对ControlNet等外部条件控制的依赖。

Result: 实验表明该方法优于现有合成方法，在保持场景结构的同时获得更高的真实感。

Conclusion: 该框架可为户外计算机视觉应用提供生成高保真训练与评估数据的稳健流水线，有助于解耦天气导致的分布偏移并提升模型跨天气鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of computer vision models, particularly in outdoor scenarios, is often limited by environmental interferences, such as adverse weather. Improving model robustness across weather conditions is thus critical for reliability. However, sourcing high-quality data relevant to isolated weather effects is a significant challenge. Most datasets include changes in dynamic elements across observations of the same scene: subjects such as pedestrians and vehicles change between shots, while camera viewpoints vary. These aspects make it difficult to decouple solely weather-induced distribution shifts from the scene. In this work, we address this with a novel weather synthesis framework that utilizes structure-retaining Low-Rank Adapters (LoRA). We analyze the attention blocks in Stable Diffusion XL (SDXL) and identify where relevant structural information is mostly processed during image-to-image translation. Through an architectural analysis of SDXL, we learn structural LoRA matrices for the corresponding blocks and then leverage them for weather synthesis, eliminating the need for external conditioning such as ControlNet. Experimental results demonstrate that our method improves upon existing synthesis approaches, achieving greater realism while preserving structure. This provides a robust pipeline for generating high-fidelity training and evaluation data for outdoor computer vision applications.

</details>


### [43] [IntReal: A Real-World Dataset and Benchmark for Multimodal Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38425)
*Shaohan Yu,Jie Qin,Wende Tan,Haiwen Diao,Limeng Qiao,Yueming Lyu,Chenyang Si*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 IntReal 基准，含 10 万真实交错图文数据（IntReal-100K）与七维度细粒度评测套件（IntReal Evaluation Suite），用于诊断统一多模态模型的交错生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类天然消费图文交错的多模态内容，但统一多模态模型（UMM）的交错生成研究不足。两大瓶颈：(1) 缺乏明确任务定义与配套训练数据；(2) 缺乏可跨能力维度可靠诊断的细粒度评测协议。

Method: 构建 IntReal-100K：覆盖四大类别的真实世界交错图文样本数据集；设计 IntReal Evaluation Suite：借助专用专家模型在七个互补维度上评测，实现由粗到细的诊断。通过指标间冗余度、与人工判断一致性、专家评测器间一致性验证评测可靠性，并对四类 UMM 家族进行基准测试及训练后实验（post-training）。

Result: 评测套件指标间冗余低（最大两两绝对 Spearman ρ≤0.42）；与人工判断一致率达 83.3%；不同专家评测器间 Spearman ρ>0.9。基准测试显示各模型在不同维度上强弱分明；后训练实验表明 IntReal-100K 可提升交错生成，且单模态生成训练亦能增益交错生成。

Conclusion: 交错生成可从模态特定优化中获益，而非每个阶段都必须进行文本-图像联合训练。

Abstract: Humans routinely consume multimodal content, where textual and visual information is naturally interleaved. Although Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) can jointly generate text and images, multimodal interleaved generation remains largely underexplored. Progress in this area is hindered by two key limitations: (1) the lack of well-defined interleaved generation tasks and corresponding training data, and (2) the absence of fine-grained evaluation protocols that can reliably diagnose model performance across different capability dimensions. To this end, we present IntReal, comprising two complementary components: IntReal-100K, a real-world dataset of multimodal interleaved samples spanning four major categories, and the IntReal Evaluation Suite, which evaluates model performance across seven complementary dimensions using specialized expert models, enabling coarse-to-fine diagnosis. Extensive experiments validate our evaluation suite through low inter-metric redundancy (max pairwise absolute Spearman's $ρ\leq 0.42$ ), high agreement with human judgments (83.3% accuracy), and strong consistency across different expert evaluators (Spearman's $ρ> 0.9$ ), supporting the complementarity and reliability of our metrics. By benchmarking four families of UMMs, we show that IntReal Evaluation Suite reveals distinct strengths and weaknesses across models. Post-training experiments demonstrate the utility of IntReal-100K for improving interleaved generation and further show that training unimodal generation can also benefit interleaved generation. These findings suggest that interleaved generation can benefit from modality-specific optimization, rather than requiring joint text-image training at every stage.

</details>


### [44] [Automatic Iconographic Annotation of Medieval Illuminations: Comparing Generative Vision-Language Models and Supervised Discriminative Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38462)
*Vincent Jolivet,Lucas Terriel,Chahan Vidal-Gor{è}ne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在9,735张整页中世纪手稿图像、2,333个TIMEL概念上比较生成式VLM与监督判别分类器（SDC）两种图像志索引范式，发现二者功能互补：SDC精度与可靠性更高，VLM语义泛化与分类学覆盖更广，并提出taxonomy-aware的soft F1指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪手稿大规模数字化催生自动化图像志索引需求；现有研究缺乏对生成式VLM与监督判别分类器在同一标注体系下的系统性、功能性对比。

Method: 在9,735张整页图像、2,333个TIMEL概念上评测两类范式：VLM侧考察微调、结构化JSON解码、基于分类学的候选生成与确定性重排序；SDC侧在冻结DINOv3编码器上对比flat、hierarchy-aware与patch-attention三类头部；并提出基于最低公共祖先、对语义相近预测给予部分credit的soft F1指标。

Result: ML-Decoder（SDC）检索性能最佳：precision@1=0.696，MRR=0.806，非对称损失（Asymmetric Loss）为主要性能杠杆；VLM经结构化JSON目标与verify-calibrate重排序取得层级F1=0.559，泛化与分类学覆盖更强；SDC擅长感知性描述概念，VLM更适于复杂解释性场景的假设生成；架构内显式层级约束反而限制覆盖，不如空间特征聚合。

Conclusion: SDC与VLM并非竞争关系，而是互补组件，宜构建结构化检索加探索性标注辅助的混合索引系统；soft F1为分类学感知评测提供了更合理的语义邻近容错。

Abstract: The large-scale digitisation of medieval manuscripts creates a growing need for automated iconographic indexing. We evaluate two complementary paradigms for this task on 9,735 full-folio images annotated with 2,333 TIMEL concepts: generative Vision-Language Models (VLM) and supervised discriminative classifiers (SDC). The VLM experiments evaluate fine-tuning, structured JSON decoding, taxonomybased candidates generation, and deterministic reranking. The discriminative experiments evaluate flat, hierarchy-aware, and patch-attention heads over a frozen DINOv3 encoder. Our results highlight key functional differences: ML-Decoder (SDC) achieves the strongest overall retrieval performance (precision@1 of 0.696, MRR of 0.806), with loss calibration (Asymmetric Loss) serving as the primary performance lever. While SDCs provide superior precision and reliability within a closed vocabulary, VLMs offer strong semantic generalization and broad taxonomic coverage, particularly through structured JSON targets and verify-calibrate reranking (hierarchical F1 of 0.559). Furthermore, model behavior strongly depends on concept abstraction: SDCs excel at perceptually grounded descriptive concepts, whereas VLMs provide useful hypotheses for complex interpretative scenes. Finally, we find that enforcing explicit hierarchy within model architectures limits coverage compared to spatial feature aggregation. Overall, rather than competing approaches, SDCs and VLMs emerge as complementary components for future hybrid indexing systems combining structured retrieval with exploratory annotation assistance. As a methodological contribution, we define the soft F1 metric to provide a taxonomy-aware evaluation that credits semantically close predictions based on lowest common ancestors.

</details>


### [45] [Geometry-Aware Trajectory State Modeling for Few-Shot Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38482)
*Zhiqi Miao,Marko Haralović,Stanislav Levendeev,Sarvesh Angadi,Jannatul Ferdous,Dan-Cristian Ploesteanu,Jiapan Guo,Estefanía Talavera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoTRAM：将动作视为2.5D轨迹状态的有序演化及其局部几何交互，用于少样本动作识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本动作识别（FSAR）需从少量标注视频中提取判别性运动动态。现有轨迹方法仅把轨迹当作采样视觉特征的载体，或用紧凑的2D位移描述子概括运动，丢失了轨迹状态的有序演化与几何交互信息。

Method: 提出几何感知的轨迹状态建模框架GeoTRAM：将稀疏轨迹提升为2.5D轨迹；StateMotion建模轨迹内有序状态演化，RelMotion通过时变相对几何与运动捕捉状态条件下的局部交互；采用质量过滤的谱采样获得更可靠轨迹初始化；对语义、运动、深度三类互补线索分别精炼并保留以联合时空建模；引入动作引导重加权与top-ρ轨迹选择，支持帧级与轨迹级互补匹配。

Result: 在五个FSAR基准上，运动主导的Something-Something系列基准取得显著提升，外观主导基准保持有竞争力性能，并具备良好精度—效率权衡。

Conclusion: 以2.5D轨迹状态演化和局部相对几何为核心的表征方式，能更充分地利用轨迹信息，为少样本动作识别提供有效且高效的建模范式。

Abstract: Few-shot action recognition (FSAR) requires representations that expose discriminative motion dynamics from only a few labeled videos. Recent trajectory-based methods leverage long-term point tracks for explicit temporal correspondence, but often treat trajectories as carriers of sampled visual features or summarize their motion with compact 2D displacement-based descriptors. We instead view an action as the ordered evolution of 2.5D trajectory states and their local geometric interactions. Based on this view, we introduce GeoTRAM, a geometry-aware trajectory state modeling framework for FSAR. GeoTRAM lifts sparse tracks into 2.5D trajectories, where StateMotion models ordered intra-trajectory state evolution and RelMotion captures state-conditioned local interactions through time-varying relative geometry and motion. Quality-filtered spectral sampling provides more reliable trajectory initialization; complementary semantic, motion, and depth cues are separately refined and preserved for joint spatio-temporal modeling; action-guided reweighting and top-$ρ$ trajectory selection support complementary frame- and trajectory-level matching. Experiments on five FSAR benchmarks demonstrate strong gains on the motion-centric Something-Something benchmarks while maintaining competitive performance on more appearance-driven benchmarks and a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [46] [Calibrating Track-Query Updates at Test Time for Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38494)
*Riku Inoue,Shogo Sato,Yu Mitsuzumi,Kazuhiko Murasaki,Tomoyasu Shimada,Takashi Sano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出免训练、免梯度的测试时轨迹查询校准方法TQC，通过抑制当前帧更新中指向邻近活跃轨迹的分量来缓解ID切换，在DanceTrack与SportsMOT九个测试设定上普遍提升HOTA与AssA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询传播式MOT跟踪器在视觉相似目标重叠或交叉时身份关联困难：当前帧查询更新可能包含指向邻近活跃轨迹的成分，从而影响后续帧的身份关联。作者通过分析IDSW前的轨迹查询更新来验证该问题。

Method: 先对IDSW前的轨迹查询更新做实证分析，发现三点规律（更新突变更大、沿其他活跃轨迹方向的投影更大、IoU可高召回检索相关轨迹）；据此提出TQC：在传播前用由指向邻近活跃轨迹的更新分量估计的有界修正来校准跟踪器输出，仅依赖推理时已有的查询表示与边界框，无需重训练或改结构。

Result: 在DanceTrack和SportsMOT的9个测试设定中，HOTA与AssA全部提升，IDF1在8个设定提升，平均HOTA提升+0.38，推理开销有限；在6个验证设定上的对照实验显示，该修正方向比等范数的替代方向带来更一致的HOTA提升。

Conclusion: IDSW部分源于查询更新沿其他活跃轨迹方向的漂移，沿该方向做有界校准是有效且轻量的免训练方案，可作为查询传播式跟踪器的即插即用改进。

Abstract: Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) requires localizing multiple objects and maintaining their identities over time. Recent query-propagation trackers carry target-specific representations across frames and update them with current-frame image features, yet identity association remains difficult when visually similar targets overlap or cross. During such interactions, current-frame query updates may contain components directed toward nearby active tracks, which may affect identity association in later frames. To investigate this issue, we analyze track-query updates preceding identity switches (IDSWs), where the same target is assigned a different track ID. Our analysis shows that (i) propagated track-query representations change more abruptly before IDSWs, (ii) current-frame updates have larger positive projections along directions toward other active tracks, and (iii) bounding-box intersection over union (IoU) retrieves the counterpart track involved in a subsequent IDSW with high recall when that track remains active before the switch. Motivated by these findings, we propose Test-time Track-Query Calibration (TQC), a training-free and gradient-free method for query-propagation trackers. TQC calibrates the tracker output before propagation using a bounded correction estimated from update components directed toward nearby active tracks. It requires only track-query representations and bounding boxes available during inference, without retraining or architectural modification. Across nine DanceTrack and SportsMOT test settings, TQC improves HOTA and AssA in all settings and IDF1 in eight, with an average HOTA gain of +0.38 and limited inference overhead. Controlled variants on six validation settings further show that TQC's correction direction yields more consistent HOTA improvements than alternative directions with matched correction norms.

</details>


### [47] [Vision-Based Density Estimation for Adaptive Traffic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38497)
*Mohammad Usaid,Deepak Silaych,Hrithik Mhatre,Archak Mittal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一个从随机视觉观测直接优化交通流的解耦框架，含车辆密度估计（CNN+自适应k-NN高斯核）、预测动力学建模（ST3DNet）与策略优化（分层Graph-Transformer MARL）三部分；目前仅完成密度估计阶段在印度斋浦尔Rambagh 4路口40张标注图像上的验证（五折MAE 3.32±0.95辆，MAPE 14.5%），预测与控制阶段尚待实证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速城市化与发展中国家交通基础设施不足使拥堵加剧；高异质、混沌交通流中严重遮挡与重叠限制了基于摄像头的交通状态估计，因此需要直接从含噪视觉观测中优化交通流。

Method: 架构结构解耦为三模块：(a) CNN学习相机画面到空间密度图的映射，采用自适应k近邻高斯核以适应不同拥堵程度；(b) ST3DNet时空预测模块；(c) 分层Graph-Transformer多智能体强化学习算法优化信号相位。

Result: 仅在感知阶段评测：40张标注路口图像，五折交叉验证MAE为3.32±0.95辆，MAPE为14.5%。

Conclusion: 密度估计模块初步可行，但结果仅限感知组件；预测与控制阶段的训练与评估留作未来工作，整体框架的端到端有效性尚未验证。

Abstract: Traffic congestion is becoming a critical challenge in rapidly growing urban centers, particularly in developing nations where rapid urbanization and inadequate transport infrastructure place increasing strain on transport networks. In highly heterogeneous and chaotic traffic flows, camera-based traffic-state estimation is limited by severe vehicle occlusion and overlap. In this paper, we propose a framework to optimize traffic flow directly from stochastic visual observations. The architecture is structurally decoupled into three primary components: (a) vehicle density estimation, (b) predictive dynamics modeling, and (c) policy optimization. The proposed methodology introduces a convolutional neural network (CNN) that learns the mapping from camera feeds to spatial density maps, utilizing an adaptive k-nearest-neighbor (k-NN) Gaussian kernel to handle varying congestion levels. This feeds into an ST3DNet predictive module for spatiotemporal forecasting, followed by a hierarchical Graph-Transformer Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) algorithm to optimize signal phases. The density estimation stage was evaluated on 40 annotated intersection images from Rambagh 4, Jaipur, India, with a reported five-fold validation mean absolute error (MAE) of $3.32 \pm 0.95$ vehicles and a mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 14.5%. These results pertain to the perception component; empirical training and evaluation of the forecasting and control stages remain future work.

</details>


### [48] [Residual Motion Splatting for Continuous Space-Time Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38499)
*Vincent Herfeld,Eva Coupet{é},Julien Mairal,Jean Ponce*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种连续空时视频超分方法，直接在学习的隐空间中按预测的亚像素位置对连续低分辨率特征做splat累加，把时间插值与多帧超分统一为单一操作，效果与速度均优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典空间多帧超分表明：以亚像素精度对齐互补观测并在更细网格上累加，可恢复超出单帧采样极限的细节。但现有学习式连续空时视频超分方法要么先在低分辨率上对齐融合再回归连续高分辨率表示，要么先把每帧插值到输出分辨率再splat；两者都在融合前重采样，导致融合时亚像素位置信息已丢失。

Method: 在学习的连续模型中恢复该原理面临两个挑战：未观测时刻的亚像素运动估计，以及粗网格向细网格splat时的空洞与遮挡。为此(1)预测逐像素连续轨迹，在全序列积分特征上对线性光流路径施加平滑残差修正；(2)直接将相邻低分辨率特征以预测的亚像素位置和可见性权重splat到高分辨率网格，再由解码器消解剩余歧义。

Result: 在多个标准基准上，重建保真度与感知质量均优于现有最先进方法，且运行速度最高提升一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过在低分辨率域保留亚像素位置并在细网格上融合，可将时间插值与多帧超分统一为单次操作，兼顾质量与效率。

Abstract: Continuous space-time video super-resolution produces a high-resolution, highframe-rate video from a low-resolution, low-frame-rate one at arbitrary spatial scales and temporal instants. Classical spatial multi-frame super-resolution has shown that detail beyond a single frame's sampling limit can be recovered by aligning complementary observations with sub-pixel precision and accumulating them on a finer grid. Existing learning-based methods largely depart from this principle: some align and fuse frames only at low resolution before regressing a continuous high-resolution representation, while others interpolate each frame to the output resolution before splatting. In both cases, the per-frame samples are resampled before fusion, so their sub-pixel positions are lost before frames are combined. Restoring this principle in a learned continuous model raises two challenges: estimating sub-pixel motion at unobserved times, and handling holes and occlusions when splatting a coarse grid onto a finer one. We address the first by predicting per-pixel continuous trajectories that refine linear optical-flow paths with smooth residual motion, using features integrated over the full video sequence. We address the second by directly splatting consecutive low-resolution features in a learned latent space onto a high-resolution grid at their predicted sub-pixel positions with predicted visibility weights, before a decoder resolves remaining ambiguities. This makes temporal interpolation and multi-frame superresolution a single operation. Our method improves both reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality on multiple standard benchmarks over the state of the art, while running up to an order of magnitude faster.

</details>


### [49] [UnMixMamba: Unmixing Guided State Space Subpixel Mapping of Crops from MODIS Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38507)
*Motasem Alkayid,Yimin Zhu,Naser El-Sheimy,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UnMixMamba，以时序-光谱-空间解耦分解、×8丰度超分与可学习端元库、以及按预测作物丰度分配Mamba token预算的自适应精化，实现MODIS 250 m时序到田块尺度AAFC标签的作物类型亚像元制图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从MODIS 250 m时序到田块尺度AAFC标签的作物类型亚像元制图（SPM）可支撑种植面积估算与农情监测，但存在三点困难：(i) 光谱-时序耦合与混合像元使粗到细分类器直接失效；(ii) 类内端元变异使单一作物单一光谱难以表征；(iii) 在高分网格上均匀精化，所有亚像元被同等对待，算力多耗于易分像元，而少数类误差仍残留。

Method: (i) 采用时序-光谱-空间解耦（TSSD）因子分解，估计单纯形分数并由同一混合场驱动AAFC分类，而非将低分观测直接映射为高分logits；(ii) 引入×8丰度超分与可学习逐作物变体端元库，在线性混合模型下重建低分与高分MODIS数据立方体；(iii) 由预测作物丰度决定固定Mamba token预算的分配位置，使高分精化聚焦少数类而非全网格。

Result: 在Alberta 2015数据、固定空间块协议下，OA 0.8397、mIoU 0.6640、AA 0.7837、Kappa 0.7715，优于ST-DRes、ESPCN、FineCrop、AgriFM及经典SPM基线。

Conclusion: UnMixMamba在已知农业掩膜内完成闭集四类作物制图，通过解耦分解、变体端元库与丰度引导的自适应精化同时缓解混合像元、端元变异与均匀精化三类问题，并在指标上超越现有方法。

Abstract: Subpixel mapping (SPM) of crop types from \SI{250}{m} Moderate Resolution Imaging Spectroradiometer (MODIS) time series to field-scale Agriculture and Agri-Food Canada (AAFC) labels supports acreage estimation and agricultural monitoring. Three difficulties remain: (i)~spectral-temporal coupling and mixed pixels that break direct coarse-to-fine classifiers; (ii)~intra-class endmember variability that a single spectrum per crop cannot capture; and (iii)~uniform refinement on the high-resolution (HR) grid, which treats every subpixel the same, so most of the effort goes to easy pixels while errors remain in the minority class. UnMixMamba addresses them as follows. For~(i), a temporal-spectral-spatial decoupled (TSSD) factorization estimates simplex fractions and drives AAFC classification from the same mixture field, instead of mapping low-resolution (LR) observations directly to HR logits. For~(ii), \(\times 8\) abundance super-resolution and a learnable per-crop variant endmember bank reconstruct the MODIS cube at LR and HR under a linear mixing model. For~(iii), the predicted crop fractions decide where a fixed Mamba token budget is spent, so HR refinement focuses on the minority class instead of the full grid. On Alberta 2015 under a fixed spatial-block protocol, UnMixMamba reaches OA 0.8397, mIoU 0.6640, AA 0.7837, and Kappa 0.7715, ahead of ST-DRes, ESPCN, FineCrop, AgriFM, and classical SPM baselines. The task is closed-set four-crop mapping inside a known agricultural mask.

</details>


### [50] [Similar Scores, Opposite Failures: Human Correction as an Instrument for Video Caption Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38511)
*Ankit Khedia,Astitva Jaiswal,Chaitanya Prasad,Anisha Raju,Aangeeras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文检验单句图像描述指标能否还原人类对稠密、多窗口视频描述的判断：答案是否定的。参考重叠类指标（BLEU-4 AUC=0.510，tf-idf 余弦 0.528）与随机水平无异；且标注粒度会显著改变错误检出结果（镜头/机位错误在逐窗口判定下为 12.8%，整段概览下仅 2.7%，相差 4.7 倍）。作者主张视频描述评测应记录“错在何处”，而非仅统计“错得多频繁”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频描述模型的评测仍普遍沿用为单句图像描述设计的参考重叠指标（BLEU、CIDEr 等）。这类指标是否仍能反映人类对稠密、多片段视频描述的判断，缺乏系统验证；同时，现有评测只给出单一标量分数，无法刻画模型失败的具体类型。

Method: 构建 196 个短片段的视频描述数据集，由 6 个前沿模型生成字幕。标注前预先固定失败分类体系（failure taxonomy），受训标注员据此对每条字幕做后编辑式判定，分别在整段概览（overview）与逐窗口（window-by-window）两种粒度下进行，并由质控审核员复核，最终获得 1,144 条概览判定与 4,672 条窗口判定。据此计算参考重叠指标的 AUC，并做多组稳健性检验与 Bonferroni 多重比较校正，另分析审查者与片段效应。

Result: 参考重叠指标无法区分被接受与被拒绝的字幕：BLEU-4 AUC=0.510，CIDEr 式 tf-idf 余弦=0.528；仅用审查者修正文本作参考时 BLEU-4 降至 0.479（95% CI 0.431–0.529），与随机不可区分。标注粒度影响检出结果：同一批标注员在概览下报告机位与镜头错误的比例为 2.7%，逐窗口下为 12.8%（4.7 倍差距，在所有稳健性检验中稳定，且在需近乎完全重写的模式上最明显）。正确概览率为 54.1%–72.7%，但 15 组两两差距中仅 4 组通过 Bonferroni 校正（校正审查者与片段效应后为 5 组）；准确率与由其派生的严重度对模型的排序不一致；准确率统计上无差异的模型具有截然不同的失败画像。

Conclusion: 单句图像描述指标不能替代对人类视频描述判断的评测；单一标量无法揭示模型间的失败结构差异。作者主张视频描述评测应记录错误类型而非仅错误频率，并公开失败分类体系、各模型失败画像、预测难度的语料属性以及可复现全部数值的代码。

Abstract: Video captioning models are increasingly evaluated with metrics designed for single-sentence image captions. We test whether those metrics recover human judgement on dense, multi-window video description, and find that they do not. Six frontier models captioned 196 short clips. Trained annotators post-edited every caption against a failure taxonomy fixed before annotation, once as a whole-clip overview and once window by window, and a quality-control reviewer checked each annotation, yielding 1,144 overview verdicts and 4,672 window verdicts. Reference-overlap metrics do not separate the captions reviewers accepted from those they rejected: BLEU-4 reaches an AUC of 0.510 and a CIDEr-style tf-idf cosine 0.528, and restricting references to reviewer corrections leaves BLEU-4 at 0.479 (95% CI 0.431-0.529), indistinguishable from chance. Review granularity changes what is detected. The same annotators record camera and shot errors on 2.7% of captions when reading the overview and on 12.8% when reading window by window, a 4.7x gap that holds across every robustness check we ran, on the mode most likely to need a near-total rewrite. Correct-overview rates span 54.1-72.7%, but only 4 of 15 pairwise gaps survive Bonferroni correction (5 after adjusting for reviewer and clip effects), and accuracy and derived severity order the models differently. Models with statistically indistinguishable accuracy have sharply different failure profiles, which no single scalar shows. We argue that caption evaluation should record what was wrong rather than only how often, and we report the taxonomy, the per-model profiles, the corpus properties that predict difficulty, and the code that regenerates every number in the paper.

</details>


### [51] [The Power Watershed as a Differentiable Segmentation Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38516)
*Galia Akl,Laurent Najman,Hugues Talbot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将分水岭变换（经典形态学分割工具）作为可微层置于深度网络末端：利用有限 p 下种子随机游走（RW）为稀疏正定线性系统、可通过一次伴随求解精确可微、其 p→∞ 极限即 Power Watershed（PW），构建"分水岭层"（WS layer）并整网端到端训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Watershed/Power Watershed 因离散、不可微而无法作为深度网络末端层被端到端训练；作者希望探究能否用其有限 p 的 RW 近似把分水岭放进网络末端并反向传播。

Method: 以种子随机游走（RW）作为 PW 的有限 p 近似：每 p 下求解稀疏正定线性系统，通过一次伴随求解获得对边权与 unary 的精确隐式梯度；多标签形式每标签一个右端项；做条件数/梯度/到极限距离随 p 变化的数值研究，并将隐式梯度与展开（unrolling）对比。在合成数据与 PanNuke 细胞核分割上评估，含二值 WS 层与以实例为标签的多标签 WS 层。

Result: 合成数据上正确边代价时 WS 层把软预测变为精确边界；PanNuke 二值 WS 层与阈值化 U-Net 持平；实例设置下实例 F1 由 0.656（U-Net+连通域）升至 0.795，与"同网络+调参标记分水岭"持平；仅用十分之一数据训练时 WS 层因有限温度"对冲"（弱化弱标记、共享争议像素）领先基线 0.015–0.020 F1。

Conclusion: 分水岭变换可经由有限 p 的 RW 求解实现端到端可微，作为网络末端层有效；隐式梯度正确且与展开一致。训练温度下得到的是学习代价的分水岭，标签数由标记而非输出布局决定。代价是推理需部署有限 p 求解而非其精确极限，并需权衡 p 的条件性与逼近程度。

Abstract: Can a Watershed transform, the classical tool of morphological segmentation, be placed at the end of a deep network and trained through? The Power Watershed (PW) is the limit of the seeded Random Walker (RW) as p grows to infinity with edge weights raised to the power p. At every finite p the RW solves a sparse positive-definite linear system and is exactly differentiable with respect to the edge weights and the unaries through one adjoint solve; its multi-label form costs one right-hand side per label, and its limit is the PW. We study this family as the last layer of a segmentation network, the watershed layer (WS layer). A conditioning study measures how the solve, the gradient and the distance to the limit evolve with p, and the implicit gradient is checked against unrolling. On synthetic data with correct edge costs the WS layer transforms a soft prediction into an exact boundary. On nuclei segmentation (PanNuke), a binary WS layer is level with a thresholded U-Net. With the instances as labels, the WS layer turns the U-Net into an instance segmenter whose number of labels is set per image by the markers rather than by the output layout, and the network is trained through that partition: instance F1 score rises from 0.656 to 0.795 over a U-Net with connected components, level with the same network followed by a tuned marker watershed. At the training temperature the partition is a watershed of the learned costs, and the finite temperature lets the solve hedge, discounting weak markers and sharing contested pixels. The hedging puts the WS layer 0.015--0.020 F1 ahead of the baseline when trained on a tenth of the data, at the price of having to deploy the finite-p solve rather than its exact limit at inference. Code and the full experimental record are released.

</details>


### [52] [Self-Consistency Geometry Injection for Multi-view Consistent Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38518)
*Yupeng Xiao,Yankai Wu,Jianjie Luo,Jiajie Lin,Yi Yu,Zhenguo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可靠性引导多视图生成框架RGMV，通过参数无关的自一致性几何门控（SCG）与可靠性感知几何注入（RGI），在多视图RGB生成中对几何引导强度进行空间自适应调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图到多视图扩散方法显式或隐式地将几何推断与纹理合成耦合，导致生成对不准确几何预测敏感；已有几何引导的解耦方法仍把预测几何当作固定条件，未显式建模其可靠性。

Method: 在多种随机采样配置下由几何提议生成器对同一参考图像与目标视角产生多组几何预测；SCG（无参数）度量其空间一致性得到可靠性图；RGI据此调制RGB合成模型中几何条件残差的注入强度。框架可适配不同几何提议生成器。

Result: 大量实验表明，可靠性感知的几何注入提升了多视图生成的几何质量；在稳定区域保留更强几何引导，并在不可靠几何线索传播至RGB生成前予以衰减。

Conclusion: 显式建模并利用几何预测的可靠性可有效缓解不准确几何对多视图生成的影响，SCG与RGI是一种通用、参数无关且可插拔的改进方案。

Abstract: Recent diffusion models have achieved impressive progress in single image to multi-view generation by synthesizing consistent novel views efficiently. Existing methods usually explicitly or implicitly couple geometry inference and texture synthesis, making generation vulnerable to inaccurate geometry prediction. Although several geometry-guided methods have explored decoupling, they usually treat predicted geometry as a fixed condition and do not explicitly model whether the geometry is reliable. We therefore propose Reliability-Guided Multi-View Generation (RGMV), which comprises a parameter-free Self-Consistency Geometry Gate (SCG) and a Reliability-aware Geometry Injection (RGI) module. Specifically, a geometry proposal generator produces multiple geometry predictions for the same reference image and target viewpoints under different stochastic sampling configurations. SCG measures their spatial agreement to derive a reliability map, which RGI uses to modulate the injection strength of geometry-conditioned residuals in the RGB synthesis model. Moreover, RGMV can adapt to different geometry proposal generators. As a result, RGMV preserves stronger geometric guidance in stable regions while attenuating unreliable geometry cues before they propagate into multi-view RGB generation. Extensive experiments show that reliability-aware geometry injection improves the geometric quality of multi-view generation. Code: https://github.com/WISLab-GDUT/RGMV.

</details>


### [53] [Detecting Fine-Grained Differences between Image Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38547)
*Leander Girrbach,Alexander von Recum,Kirill Bykov,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了用MLLM生成概念与用细粒度patch嵌入从候选列表中筛选概念这两种范式，发现后者能以更低成本替代前者，并将MLLM推理预算转用于优化已选概念以获得显著提升；同时提出针对低 prevalence 与多概念区分的新基准，强调应选择"非冗余"概念而非仅最具区分性的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测两个数据分布是否不同、以及具体在哪些概念上不同，是基础统计问题，可应用于比较两类样本的概念差异、不同生成模型输出差异、同一模型不同提示下的差异。现有工作习惯用MLLM提出概念、再用图像级嵌入进行选择，成本高；作者怀疑更合理的工作流应是先用细粒度patch嵌入从宽泛概念列表中筛选，再用MLLM优化概念。

Method: 1) 对两种技术路线做系统性对比：MLLM概念提议 + 图像级嵌入选择 vs. 宽列表 + patch级嵌入选择；2) 将节省下的MLLM推理用于优化已选概念；3) 设计新基准，聚焦低 prevalence（低频出现）与多概念区分场景；4) 提出选择非冗余概念（而非仅最具区分性概念）的具体技术；5) 应用于虚假相关与偏见检测。

Result: 从宽泛列表中筛选概念可替代MLLM提议，成本明显更低；将MLLM推理转用于概念优化可带来实质性提升；在低 prevalence 与多概念区分基准上，选择非冗余概念优于仅选最具区分性概念；所提方法可有效检测虚假相关与偏见。

Conclusion: 论文提出一种关于"如何分析数据集间可解释分布差异"的根本性视角转变：以patch级嵌入驱动的宽列表筛选承担概念发现，以MLLM承担概念精炼，并重视概念的非冗余性；该框架在成本效率与低 prevalence、多概念场景下均具优势。

Abstract: Detecting whether and in particular how two data distributions differ is a fundamental statistical problem. For example, we might be interested in which concepts (expressed in natural language) differ between two classes, the outputs of different generative models, or the same model with different prompt specifications. When working with large and complex image datasets, we intuitively think that first selecting concepts from a broad list using fine-grained patch embeddings, and then optimizing the concepts to better fit the data is an effective workflow. However, prior work instead uses MLLMs to propose concepts and image-level embeddings to select them. Therefore, we systematically compare these techniques and find that selecting concepts from broad lists can replace MLLM-proposals at obviously lower cost. MLLM inference can then be spent on optimizing selected concepts instead to achieve substantial improvements. Additionally, by designing a new benchmark that focuses on low-prevalence and multi-concept distinctions, we find that selecting nonredundant concepts instead of only the most distinguishing concepts matters, and we introduce techniques for how to effectively do this. Finally, we show how our methods address important problems like detecting spurious correlations and biases. Overall, our work presents a fundamental perspective shift on how we analyze interpretable distributional differences between datasets.

</details>


### [54] [RoSAMDepth: Robust Self-supervised Depth Estimation Leveraging Segment Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38587)
*Xuanang Gao,Zhiwei Ning,Gengming Zhang,Jiaxi Cao,Runze Yang,Zhonglong Zheng,Jie Yang,Rong Xiao,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoSAMDepth框架，利用SAM的物体级先验增强鲁棒自监督深度估计，在数据集与恶劣天气下均优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度估计方法未利用物体级信息，导致预测深度在物体内部易偏离、恶劣条件下易模糊。

Method: 从三方面引入SAM的物体级先验：1) 分段引导的表示对比方法，将物体级感知注入特征表示空间；2) 自适应区域离群点掩码策略结合区域高斯似然损失，强化区域深度平滑；3) 物体级可靠性估计策略，降低不可靠监督的影响。

Result: 在多数据集和多种天气条件下实验表明，方法生成更清晰、更准确的深度预测，持续优于SOTA。

Conclusion: 借助SAM的物体级先验可有效提升自监督深度估计的鲁棒性与精度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Robust depth estimation aims to maintain high-quality depths across diverse conditions. However, most existing methods estimate depth without taking into account the object-level information. As a result, the predicted depth may easily deviate within objects and become blurred under adverse conditions. To overcome this weakness, we propose RoSAMDepth, a novel framework that can assist robust self-supervised depth estimation in leveraging rich and diverse object-level priors from the Segment Anything Model (SAM). We focus on incorporating object-level information across three key aspects: a segment-guided representation contrasting method that injects object-level awareness into the feature representation space; an adaptive regional outlier masking strategy combined with a regional Gaussian likelihood loss that enforces regional depth smoothness; and an object-level reliability estimation strategy that mitigates the influence of unreliable supervision. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets and diverse weather conditions demonstrate that our method produces sharper, more accurate depth predictions, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/xagao/RoSAMDepth.

</details>


### [55] [SpectralTF-MAE: A Pre-trained Spectral Foundation Model for Tokamak Fusion Plasma Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38593)
*Xiao Wang,Zhijie Lu,Duoqing Yang,Jiangtao Yu,Jin Tang,Zichao Lin,Hongming Zhang,Guosheng Xu,Bo Lyu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpectralTF-MAE，一个面向托卡马克等离子体光谱诊断的自监督光谱基础模型：在成对的 CXRS 与 XCS 数据上通过掩码光谱重建预训练，采用峰值感知掩码与峰值增强重建目标，并用共享 Transformer 编码器学习跨模态可迁移表征，随后适配参数预测、跨诊断光谱生成与谱峰分析等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光谱诊断能提供丰富的等离子体状态信息，但现有基于学习的方法多为单任务设计，且高度依赖标注数据，缺乏可通用的跨诊断表征。

Method: 构建掩码自编码（MAE）式自监督框架：以电荷交换复合光谱（CXRS）与 X 射线晶体光谱（XCS）配对测量为预训练数据，做掩码光谱重建；引入峰值感知掩码策略（优先遮蔽特征谱区）与峰值增强重建目标以强化关键光谱结构；共享 Transformer 编码器实现跨诊断模态的迁移表征；预训练后微调至多个下游任务。

Result: 在 EAST 诊断数据上，各下游任务（等离子体参数预测、跨诊断光谱生成、谱峰分析）均取得有竞争力的性能，且一致地从自监督光谱预训练中获益。

Conclusion: SpectralTF-MAE 可作为等离子体光谱诊断的通用、可迁移表征学习框架，支持多诊断多任务统一建模并降低对标注数据的依赖。

Abstract: Spectroscopic diagnostics provide rich information for characterizing plasma states in tokamak fusion experiments, while existing learning-based approaches are often designed for individual tasks and rely heavily on labeled data. In this work, we propose SpectralTF-MAE, a self-supervised spectral foundation model for tokamak plasma diagnostics. The model is pretrained on paired charge-exchange recombination spectroscopy (CXRS) and X-ray crystal spectroscopy (XCS) measurements through masked spectral reconstruction. To better capture diagnostically important spectral structures, we introduce a peak-aware masking strategy together with a peak-enhanced reconstruction objective, encouraging the model to focus on characteristic spectral regions. A shared Transformer encoder is employed to learn transferable representations across different diagnostic modalities. The pretrained model is further adapted to several downstream tasks, including plasma-parameter prediction, cross-diagnostic spectral generation, and spectral peak analysis. Experiments on EAST diagnostic data demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves competitive performance across different tasks and consistently benefits from self-supervised spectral pretraining. These results indicate the potential of SpectralTF-MAE as a general and transferable representation learning framework for plasma spectroscopic diagnostics.

</details>


### [56] [Data Scaling Laws in Stereo Matching: From In-domain Fitting to Cross-domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38600)
*Tong Zhao,Di Liang,Yiming Zeng,Bo Xu,Yingzhi Tang,Chen Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文初步研究立体匹配中域内与跨域的数据缩放律，构建20万样本的真实感合成数据集XStereo，发现域内训练随数据量呈两阶段缩放（幂律→饱和），且临界转折点对固定模型近似不变；跨域性能对源域数据规模的依赖显著更弱且更不可预测，分布与覆盖多样性更有效缩小域间差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型时代立体匹配趋向于用百万级合成图像训练的通用基础模型，但模型性能如何随合成数据规模扩展尚缺乏系统研究；现有缩放律主要在语言与视觉任务中验证，立体匹配领域的数据缩放规律未被探索。

Method: 构建名为XStereo的真实感合成数据集（20万样本）作为数据缩放研究的高质量基准；在五个大规模数据集上进行域内训练，拟合性能-数据量关系；并分析跨域泛化性能与源域数据规模的关系，考察分布与覆盖多样性对域间差距的影响。

Result: 域内训练呈现两阶段缩放行为，从标准幂律过渡到饱和，且在固定模型下临界转折点近似不变；跨域性能对源域数据规模的依赖明显更弱、更不可预测；数据分布与覆盖多样性比单纯扩大规模更能缓解域间差距。

Conclusion: 研究对语言与视觉中已有缩放律进行了验证与扩展，并为高效数据构建与训练提供了可操作建议，有望推动更高效、更强的立体匹配模型发展；XStereo数据集已公开。

Abstract: In the era of large models, stereo matching is developing towards generalizable foundation models trained on million-scale synthetic images. However, how model performance scales up with synthetic data is still underexplored. In this work, we preliminarily investigate the in-domain and cross-domain data scaling laws in stereo matching. A realistic synthetic dataset, named XStereo, containing 200 thousand samples is first built to serve as a high-quality benchmark for data scaling research. In-domain training on five large-scale datasets demonstrate a two-regime scaling behavior with data, transitioning from the standard power law to saturation, where the critical transition point is approximately invariant under a fixed model. Cross-domain performance exhibits a substantially weaker and less predictable dependence on the source-domain data scale. The distribution and coverage diversity are more effective for diminishing domain gaps. More instructive suggestions are provided for high-efficiency data construction and training. The insightful findings are significant verification and extension of the existing scaling laws in both language and vision, paving the way to efficient and stronger stereo matching. The XStereo dataset is public at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Tong-Zhao/XStereo.

</details>


### [57] [V-CAPE: Visual Consistency Assessment for Product Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38611)
*Iaroslav Melekhov,Roberto Alcover Couso,Francesco Taioli,Daniel Coelho,Artur Bekasov,Virginia Fernandez Arguedas,Jose Miguel Grande Saiz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V-CAPE数据集，用于评估生成式图像编辑中的视觉一致性，包含4万对产品源图-目标图、二分类接受/拒绝标签及细粒度失败模式标注，现有VLM准确率低于65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像编辑可实现背景替换、物体操作、姿态调整等复杂变换，但验证变换是否保持物体的内在视觉属性（几何、纹理、颜色、细节）仍基本未解决；大规模场景下人工检查不可行，需自动化质量评估（AutoQA）。

Method: 构建V-CAPE（Visual Consistency Assessment for Product Image Editing）大规模数据集：40K独特源-目标产品图像对，每对标注二分类接受/拒绝标签与结构化失败模式（几何畸变、姿态错误、身份不匹配等）；结合真实编辑图像与基于3D资产生成的合成子集，兼顾真实性与可控分析；在AutoQA任务上评测多种规模类别的视觉语言模型。

Result: 所有规模类别的VLM在AutoQA上准确率均低于65%，表明该数据集无论模型规模如何均具显著挑战性，揭示当前模型在验证变换下视觉一致性方面的根本局限。

Conclusion: V-CAPE可作为推进细粒度图像编辑评估的基准，为视觉一致性验证研究提供规模化、可控的评测平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative image editing enable complex transformations such as background replacement, object manipulation, and pose adjustment from natural language instructions. However, verifying that these transformations preserve visual consistency, defined as the preservation of an object's intrinsic visual attributes, including geometry, texture, color, and fine-grained details, remains a largely unsolved problem. This challenge is particularly pronounced at scale, where manual inspection is infeasible and automated quality assessment (AutoQA) could become essential. In this work, we introduce Visual Consistency Assessment for Product Image Editing (V-CAPE), a large-scale dataset for evaluating visual consistency in generative image editing. The dataset consists of 40K unique source-target product image pairs, each annotated with binary accept/reject labels and structured failure modes capturing fine-grained inconsistencies such as geometric distortions, pose errors, and identity mismatches. V-CAPE combines real-world edited images with a synthetic subset constructed from 3D assets, enabling both realistic evaluation and controlled analysis. Vision-language models (VLMs) across all evaluated size categories on AutoQA achieve accuracy below 65%, indicating that the dataset poses a substantial challenge regardless of model scale. Our results highlight fundamental limitations of current models in verifying visual consistency under transformation, positioning V-CAPE as a benchmark for advancing fine-grained image editing evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [TRIM: Temporal Run-Time Monitoring of 3D Object Detection via Activation Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38613)
*Hakan Yekta Yatbaz,Konstantinos Koufos,Mehrdad Dianati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRIM是一种轻量级时序运行时监控器，通过通道统计量+时序卷积网络检测3D目标检测器的漏检失败，无需修改或重训检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在智能交通感知中会静默漏检安全关键目标却仍输出貌似合理的预测；现有3D检测运行时监控器逐帧独立评估，无法利用遮挡、点稀疏与场景复杂度的时间演化信息。

Method: TRIM用紧凑的通道级统计量概括检测器中间激活，并用时序卷积网络（TCN）处理这些摘要构成的短序列；监控器外挂于原检测器，不修改、不重训。

Result: 在NuScenes上，相比单帧监控，时序监控将AUROC从0.686提升至0.704，并将95%召回下的误报率从0.818降至0.759；骨干网络早期与中间层特征提供最强监控信号，时序推理头在CPU上仅需0.159 ms。

Conclusion: 时序建模可有效提升漏检失败检测的判别力并降低误报，且代价极低、易部署，适用于实时智能交通系统。

Abstract: Reliable perception is essential to intelligent transportation systems, yet deep neural networks can silently miss safety-critical objects while continuing to produce apparently plausible outputs. Existing run-time monitors for 3D object detection generally assess each frame independently and therefore cannot exploit the temporal evolution of occlusion, point sparsity, and scene complexity. We present TRIM, a lightweight temporal monitor that detects missed-object failures without modifying or retraining the underlying detector. TRIM summarises intermediate activations using compact channel-wise statistics and processes short sequences of these summaries with a temporal convolutional network. Experiments on NuScenes show that temporal monitoring improves AUROC from 0.686 to 0.704 and reduces the false alarm rate at 95% recall from 0.818 to 0.759 compared with single-frame monitoring. Early and intermediate backbone features provide the strongest monitoring signal, while the temporal inference head requires only 0.159 ms on a CPU.

</details>


### [59] [Cross-Primitive Consistency in Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38623)
*Xu Liu,Guikun Chen,Kanzhi Wu,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出四轴基准 PrimaCons，系统评估 Jev 中 LLM 跨查询原语（如二分类与多选）决策一致性，发现各轴均存在违反且呈系统模式（不一致可预测失败，ROC-AUC 0.91），并提出 CPRC 校准器降低不一致性 58.57%、提升准确率 2.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jev 通过类型化查询原语使同一语义状态可用不同格式（二分类、多选等）表达以支持高效决策，但跨原语的一致性从未被系统评估，而一致性缺失可能损害决策可靠性。

Method: 构建 PrimaCons 四轴基准，在保持查询语义不变的前提下测量跨原语一致性；在三种 Jev 规模上评测并分析不一致模式；提出 Cross-Primitive Residual Calibrator（CPRC），在冻结主干上附加轻量残差网络，联合优化预测准确率与跨原语一致性，并采用留一领域（leave-one-domain-out）协议验证。

Result: 四个轴均观察到一致性违反，且违反呈系统性模式；跨原语差异可预测决策失败（ROC-AUC 0.91）；CPRC 使跨原语不一致性降低 58.57%，准确率提升 +2.97%，在域内与域外均有效。

Conclusion: 跨原语一致性应作为评估 Jev 的关键维度；CPRC 为提升其决策可靠性提供了实用方案，并公开代码与基准。

Abstract: Jev enables efficient decision-making with large language models (LLMs) through typed querying primitives, allowing the same semantic state to be expressed in different formats, such as binary and multiple-choice queries. However, the consistency of decisions across primitives has not been systematically evaluated. This work introduces PrimaCons, a four-axis benchmark for assessing cross-primitive consistency while preserving the semantics of the query. Evaluations across three Jev sizes reveal consistency violations across all four axes. Our analysis shows that these violations follow systematic patterns and that cross-primitive discrepancies predict decision failure with a ROC-AUC of 0.91. To mitigate these inconsistencies, we propose the Cross-Primitive Residual Calibrator (CPRC), which attaches a lightweight residual network to a frozen backbone and jointly optimizes predictive accuracy and cross-primitive consistency. Under a leave-one-domain-out protocol, CPRC reduces cross-primitive inconsistency by 58.57% and improves accuracy by +2.97%., demonstrating its effectiveness in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings. Our findings establish cross-primitive consistency as a key dimension for evaluating Jev and provide a practical approach to improving the reliability of its decisions. Code and benchmark is available at https://github.com/67L1/PrimaCons-CRPR.

</details>


### [60] [AskStream: When and What to Ask Proactively under Uncertainty in Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38678)
*Lu Wang,Zhuoran Jin,Longxiang Wang,Junhong Chen,Yupu Hao,Yao Xu,Daojian Zeng,Yubo Chen,Kang Liu,Guang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将主动交互范式扩展为"等待/提问/回答"三类动作，并构建首个覆盖视频侧与用户侧不确定性的多轮基准AskStream（438个视频、2714个帧级时间窗、4类任务8个子任务），最强模型Ask-F1仅52.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在视频理解中的主动交互范式假设回答所需信息是完整的，模型只能在"等待"与"回答"之间选择。但现实中信息常因视频本身或用户的不确定性而不完整，导致模型无限等待或产生严重幻觉。因此需要引入第三类动作——主动提问，以获取缺失信息。

Method: 将主动交互重新定义为包含wait/ask/answer的三种动作；构建AskStream多轮基准，涵盖视频不确定性与用户不确定性两类来源，包含4个任务类别、8个子任务，共438个视频与2714个人工标注的帧级时间窗（标注类型为wait、ask、answer）；在多项选择与开放式两种设定下评测"何时提问"与"提问什么"，并提出Ask-F1指标。

Result: 即使是最强模型，在多项选择与开放式设定下的Ask-F1也仅为52.18%，表明模型在不确定性下的主动信息寻求能力存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 不确定性条件下的主动信息寻求是流式视频理解的关键未解问题；将"提问"纳入主动交互范式并配套AskStream基准，为后续研究提供了明确的问题定义与评测基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models have increased interest in interaction paradigms for video understanding. The evolution from offline to streaming and proactive interaction has gradually shifted models from passive responders toward more proactive agents that can decide when to answer. However, existing proactive paradigms typically assume that the information required to answer a query is complete, leaving models to only wait or answer. In the real world, however, information is often incomplete due to uncertainty from either the video or the user. In proactive paradigms, such uncertainty can cause the model to wait forever or generate highly hallucinatory responses. We therefore argue that proactive interaction should be redefined with a third action: ask, allowing models to actively ask for missing information. To study when and what models should ask before answering, we introduce AskStream, the first multi-turn benchmark spanning video and user uncertainty for streaming video understanding, with four task categories and eight subtasks. It contains 438 videos with 2,714 manually annotated frame-level time windows across three types: wait, ask, and answer. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended settings, even the strongest model attains only 52.18% on our proposed Ask-F1 metric, highlighting substantial limitations in proactive information seeking under uncertainty.

</details>


### [61] [Geometry Matters in Packed 3D Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38694)
*Amil Khan,Anantajit Subrahmanya,B. S. Manjunath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACK3D提出局部-全局注意力算子，用真实token网格坐标上的轴向RoPE解耦"内存布局"与"注意力几何"，解决3D体数据打包成序列后位置机制学到的是打包顺序而非物体几何的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将3D体积打包为token序列不仅是长序列建模问题：打包后序列顺序不再编码样本几何，按序列位置索引的位置机制（学习式位置嵌入、打包序列RoPE）可能学到内存布局而非物体本身。这是注意力处理打包几何数据的通用失效模式，论文旨在直接刻画并解决它。

Method: 构造受控合成任务：固定物理标注规则、只改变token内存顺序，对比学习式位置嵌入、打包序列RoPE与轴向网格坐标RoPE。方法上保持局部邻域注意力精确；仅将远距离上下文压缩为连续的3D块摘要；每条query-key交互均以真实token网格坐标上的轴向RoPE索引；由于旋转在块摘要之前施加，query到摘要的logit仍为query相对3D位移的加权和，压缩后的全局上下文保持空间可解释性。另提供融合Triton实现。

Result: 合成任务中，学习式位置嵌入与打包序列RoPE完美拟合训练布局，但在Morton、块连续、随机重打包下全部崩溃，轴向网格坐标RoPE始终精确。在Allen WTC-11 hiPSC显微数据15结构联合任务上，PACK3D优于参数匹配的3D U-Net及匹配的学习位置局部-全局基线，消融控制将增益归于坐标系而非编码器容量。Triton实现在65k token下匹配窗口注意力延迟，同时提供窗口化无法获得的长程上下文。

Conclusion: 打包几何数据的位置机制必须基于几何坐标而非序列位置；PACK3D在保持局部精确性的同时获得空间可解释的压缩全局上下文，兼具正确性与大规模可训练性。

Abstract: Packing a 3D volume into a token sequence is not merely long-sequence modeling. Once the volume is packed, sequence order no longer encodes the geometry of the sample---so a positional mechanism indexed by sequence position can learn the memory layout rather than the object. This is a general failure mode for attention over packed geometric data. We introduce PACK3D, a local-global attention operator that separates memory layout from attention geometry. PACK3D keeps local neighborhood attention exact, compresses only distant context into contiguous 3D block summaries, and indexes every query-key interaction with axial RoPE on true token-grid coordinates rather than packed positions. Because the rotation is applied before a block is summarized, the query-to-summary logit remains a weighted sum of query-relative 3D displacements; compressed global context therefore stays spatially interpretable after packing, instead of collapsing to anonymous pooled content. We measure the failure mode directly. On a controlled synthetic task---train on one packing, change only the memory order of the tokens, hold the physical labeling rule fixed---learned positional embeddings and packed-sequence RoPE solve the training layout perfectly yet collapse under Morton, block-contiguous, and random repacking, while axial grid-coordinate RoPE stays exact throughout. The same coordinate system carries to real data: on Allen WTC-11 hiPSC microscopy, PACK3D improves over a parameter-matched 3D U-Net on the 15-structure joint task and over a matched learned-position local-global baseline, with controls isolating the coordinate system, not encoder capacity, as the source of the gain. A fused Triton implementation matches windowed-attention latency at 65k tokens while supplying the long-range context windowing cannot---so the operator is not only correct but trainable at scale.

</details>


### [62] [inkGround: Benchmarking Fine-grained Visual Grounding Capabilities of Vision-Language Models for Cursive Handwriting in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38720)
*Sidra Hanif*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 inkGround 基准：从整页拍摄手写图像恢复有序笔迹轨迹，将任务表述为文本条件点定位，并系统评测 7 个通用 VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线手写图像只保留最终墨迹，丢失了笔画书写顺序与抬/落笔信息；而现有图像-轨迹配对数据集由数字化板在受控条件下采集，书写者覆盖与风格多样性受限，且缺乏衡量通用视觉语言模型笔迹轨迹恢复视觉定位能力的基准。

Method: 构建 inkGround 基准：537 张整页相机拍摄图像（约 18.7k 词、约 163k 笔画）与预训练 offline-to-online 模型生成的参考轨迹配对，经 OCR 一致性筛选与人工核验；因参考为伪标签，另在含真实记录轨迹的 IAM-OnDB 上评测。任务定义为文本条件点定位：给定整页图像与目标词，模型需定位该词并输出有序笔画点。评测指标包括轨迹保真度（DTW、Chamfer 距离）、渲染笔画可读性（Accurate Rate、Correct Rate）与风格相似度。用统一 in-context prompt 评测 4 个开源与 3 个前沿闭源 VLM。

Result: 闭源模型（尤其 Gemini-3.1-Pro）生成的轨迹可读且接近参考轨迹，明显优于开源模型；该排名在 IAM-OnDB 上同样成立，说明结论不依赖于伪标签生成方式。

Conclusion: inkGround 为首个面向整页拍摄手写的笔迹轨迹恢复基准，并首次系统评估通用 VLM 在该任务上的视觉定位能力；闭源前沿模型已具备较强轨迹恢复能力，但开源模型仍有明显差距，伪标签与真实记录双轨评测可提升结论可信度。

Abstract: Human handwriting is produced as an ordered sequence of pen movements, but an offline image keeps only the final ink. Handwriting trajectory recovery aims to restore the ordered stroke points and their pen-up/pen-down transitions. Existing image-trajectory pair datasets are recorded with digitizing tablets under controlled conditions, which limits writer coverage and stylistic diversity. Moreover, there is no benchmark that measures the visual grounding capabilities of general-purpose Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for the stroke trajectory recovery task. In this work, we introduce inkGround, a benchmark for stroke trajectory recovery from full-page, camera-captured handwriting. inkGround pairs 537 page images (~18.7k words, ~163k strokes) with reference trajectories produced by a pretrained offline-to-online model, filtered by OCR agreement, and verified by human raters. Because these references are pseudo-labels that represent one plausible pen trajectory, we also evaluate VLMs on the IAM-OnDB dataset, which provides recorded pen trajectories. We cast the task as text-conditioned point grounding: given a page image and a target word, the model must locate the word and output its ordered stroke points. Outputs are scored by trajectory fidelity (DTW, Chamfer distance), readability of the rendered strokes (Accurate Rate, Correct Rate), and style similarity. We benchmark four open-source and three frontier closed-source VLMs with a shared in-context prompt. Closed-source models, notably Gemini-3.1-Pro, produce legible trajectories close to the reference, and this ranking also holds on IAM-OnDB.

</details>


### [63] [RuleForge: Inferring and Instantiating Latent Visual Rules as Executable Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38737)
*Weifeng Xu,Yaxiong Wang,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Bongard-Forge100k 基准与 RuleForge 框架，联合学习视觉规则理解与规则条件下的可执行程序生成，显著提升多模态模型在熟悉规则与held-out规则组合上的推理与实例化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究或仅从视觉样例推断规则但只评测分类/语言表述，或在规则作为输入的前提下监督可执行程序生成，导致"视觉规则理解"与"可靠规则实例化"之间的关系缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建受控基准 Bongard-Forge100k（108,797题：86,000训练、19,297熟悉规则测试、3,500 held-out规则组合测试），并提出与骨干无关的 RuleForge 框架，分三阶段训练：规则理解训练；理解与场景程序联合监督；基于执行反馈的强化学习精炼（含 Understanding Replay）。以多随机种子执行生成程序，获得可执行性、规则满足度、多样性与鲁棒性的可验证反馈。

Result: 以 UniLIP 为主骨干，RuleForge 相较基模型在熟悉规则上多选题准确率提升 20.93 个百分点，在 held-out 规则组合上提升 28.42 个百分点；熟悉规则下场景级规则满足度从 2.91% 升至 70.99%。在 InternVL3.5、Qwen3-VL、Show-o2 上的实验验证了训练数据与框架的跨架构有效性。

Conclusion: 视觉规则理解与可执行规则实例化可以协同学习，执行反馈既可作为可验证奖励信号，也能跨多种多模态架构泛化，为视觉规则推理提供了可控评测与统一训练范式。

Abstract: Visual rule reasoning asks whether multimodal models can recover an abstract concept from a few examples. Existing studies either infer rules from visual examples but evaluate only classification or verbalization, or supervise executable generation with rules provided as input. Consequently, the relationship between visual rule understanding and reliable rule instantiation remains insufficiently explored. We present Bongard-Forge100k, a controlled benchmark, together with RuleForge, a backbone-agnostic framework that jointly learns visual rule understanding and rule-conditioned executable generation. The benchmark contains 108,797 problems: 86,000 for training, 19,297 for testing familiar rules, and 3,500 for testing held-out rule compositions. The framework progresses through rule-understanding training, joint understanding and scene-program supervision, and refinement via execution-grounded reinforcement learning with Understanding Replay. Executing generated programs under multiple random seeds provides verifiable feedback on executability, rule satisfaction, diversity, and robustness. With UniLIP as the primary backbone, RuleForge improves multiple-choice accuracy over the base model by 20.93 percentage points on familiar rules and 28.42 percentage points on held-out rule compositions, while increasing scene-level rule satisfaction on familiar rules from 2.91% to 70.99%. Additional experiments on InternVL3.5, Qwen3-VL, and Show-o2 demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed training data and framework across diverse multimodal architectures.

</details>


### [64] [A 211K Parameter Foundation Model: Genetic Prompting in Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38776)
*John Kalkhof,Niklas Ihm,Daniel Kirste,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量基础模型Foundational-NCA，基于神经细胞自动机与遗传提示机制，以仅21.1万参数实现多任务医学分割的免重训练自适应，参数量比SAM类模型少180倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础模型虽具备广泛任务泛化能力，但医学影像分割需求因临床场景差异极大；SAM等模型以可适应性换取规模，依赖高端算力，使最需要诊断支持的低资源环境被排除在外。作者旨在弥合这一差距，构建可在低基础设施条件下部署的轻量医学分割基础模型。

Method: Foundational-NCA以神经细胞自动机（NCA）为骨干，在潜在任务空间上结合基于梯度的学习与遗传优化，用紧凑的任务向量引导预测；通过遗传提示（genetic prompt）机制实现动态任务自适应，无需重训练即可跨任务泛化，参数量仅211K。

Result: 在TotalSegmentator数据集的15个已训练任务与6个完全未见任务上评估，平均Dice分别为84.5%与60.4%；参数量比38.9M–636M的SAM类模型低180倍以上。

Conclusion: 结果表明超紧凑模型也能实现任务泛化，为基础设施受限的临床环境提供了资源高效、可部署的基础模型路径，推动医学AI向更公平普惠的方向发展。

Abstract: The scale of modern foundation models enables broad task generalization. In medical imaging, where segmentation demands vary widely across clinical contexts, such flexibility is invaluable. But current models, such as SAM trade adaptability for scale, locking their utility behind high-end infrastructure and leaving behind the low-resource environments most in need of diagnostic support. To bridge this gap, we introduce Foundational-NCA, a lightweight foundation model capable of adapting to a wide range of medical segmentation tasks without retraining. Built on Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), Foundational-NCA incorporates gradient-based learning with genetic optimization over a latent task space, using a compact task vector to guide predictions. This enables dynamic adaptation via a genetic prompt mechanism, allowing the model to generalize across tasks while keeping the parameter count magnitudes lower at just 211 thousand, compared to SAM-based models with 38.9 to 636 million parameters. We evaluate Foundational-NCA on 15 trained and 6 entirely unseen segmentation tasks from the TotalSegmentator dataset, achieving an average Dice score of 84.5% on known tasks and 60.4% on unseen ones. These results demonstrate that task generalization is possible in ultra-compact models, laying the groundwork for resource-efficient, deployable foundation models in clinical environments where infrastructure is limited. By reducing model size by over 180 times, Foundational-NCA takes a step toward a more equitable future for medical AI, one where powerful tools are within reach of every healthcare setting.

</details>


### [65] [Beyond Region Memory: Local Point Tracking for Vocal Cord Localization in Video Laryngoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38830)
*Huayu Wang,Gyungmin Ko,Jung Heon Kim,Jia-Xian Jian,Dae Kon Kim,Dong Keon Lee,Hsiang-Wei Huang,Pau-Choo Chung,Linda Shapiro,Richard B. Utarnachitt,Jenq-Neng Hwang,Nathan J. White*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出局部点跟踪框架LPT，以“先定位、后吸附”流程实现紧急视频喉镜下的鲁棒声带定位与分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急视频喉镜中声带定位面临快速组织形变、镜面高光和器械遮挡，传统有状态掩码跟踪易受遮挡与掩码误差累积影响，难以稳定定位。

Method: LPT由三部分组成：单帧语义检测器生成稀疏锚框并初始化局部查询点（默认SAM支持，正则网格为后备）；点传播模块跨检测漏检帧跟踪这些点，并由点几何恢复时序边界框；随后通过box-snap步骤将粗框精化至目标边界。时序状态存于局部点坐标而非有状态掩码，空间精化不回传掩码误差至时序记忆。

Result: 在两个不同数据集上的大量实验表明，LPT整体性能优于现有基线，在两个数据集上均取得最佳mAP_50、success AUC和最低missing rate。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实点跟踪主要提升时序跟踪鲁棒性，box-snap精化则有效提升边界紧致度；解耦的“点时序+单帧空间精化”设计有效。

Abstract: Reliable vocal-cord localization in emergency video laryngoscopy remains visually challenging due to rapid tissue deformation, specular highlights, and instrument occlusions. We propose Local Point Tracking (LPT), a robust framework that combines a single-frame detector, local point propagation, and stateless mask refinement. LPT follows a localize first, snap second pipeline. First, a single-frame semantic detector generates sparse anchors, within which LPT initializes local query points (SAM-supported by default, with a regular grid as fallback). A point-propagation module tracks these points through detector-missed frames and recovers temporal bounding boxes from their geometry. Second, because point-derived boxes can be loose around the visible boundary, a box-snap step refines the selected box to the target boundary. This decomposition keeps temporal state in local point coordinates rather than a stateful mask or whole-region representation, while the spatial refinement module handles single-frame boundary precision without feeding mask errors back into temporal memory. Extensive evaluations on two distinct datasets demonstrate that our proposed LPT method achieves strong overall performance against existing baselines, with the best mAP_50, success AUC, and missing rate on both datasets. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm that point tracking primarily enhances temporal tracking robustness, while box-snap refinement effectively improves boundary tightness.

</details>


### [66] [OneScene-Bench: Do Vision-Language Models Integrate Evidence Across Views?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38926)
*Swathi Suhas,Anurag Das,Bernt Schiele,Jonas Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OneScene-Bench受控基准，显式区分VLM何时必须进行跨视角整合，揭示模型"处理多图"与"整合多图证据"之间存在显著鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM越来越多地处理同一场景的多视角图像，但能否将其整合为一致的场景级表征尚不清晰；现有多图评测未隔离该能力，任务可借助逐视角独立处理即得正确答案，无需跨视角对应。

Method: 构建真实世界多视角受控基准OneScene-Bench，评测三项互补能力：唯一物体计数（通过控制视角间物体重叠，区分逐视角聚合即可解决的情形与需实例级对应的情形）、跨视角一致性（在共享区域与视角特有区域引入局部编辑，检验模型能否区分真实场景矛盾与由视角、可见性引起的差异）、空间推理（构造需跨视角整合空间证据以比较物理距离的物体关系）。评测12个最先进的开源权重与专有VLM。

Result: 互补视角下计数准确率大幅下降至4.5–50.0%，而完全重叠样本为45.5–81.5%；跨视角一致性在各模型（含最新专有VLM）上仅约50%，尽管空间推理表现明显更强。

Conclusion: VLM在处理多张图像与跨图像整合证据之间仍存在实质差距，凸显需要显式测试场景级多视角理解的评测，而非可被逐视角独立处理所满足的任务。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly process multiple views of the same scene, but it remains unclear whether they integrate these observations into a coherent scene-level representation. Existing multi-image evaluations do not isolate this capability, as tasks may admit correct solutions through independent per-view processing without requiring cross-view correspondence. We introduce OneScene-Bench, a controlled benchmark designed to explicitly distinguish when cross-view integration is necessary. Our benchmark evaluates three complementary capabilities on real-world multi-view scenes: unique object counting, cross-view consistency, and spatial reasoning. For counting, we control object overlap across views to separate cases solvable by simple per-view aggregation from those requiring instance-level correspondence. For consistency, we introduce localized edits in shared and view-specific regions, testing whether models distinguish genuine scene contradictions from differences caused by viewpoint and visibility. For spatial reasoning, we construct object relations that require combining spatial evidence across viewpoints to compare physical proximity. We evaluate twelve state-of-the-art open-weight and proprietary VLMs and find that counting accuracy drops substantially on complementary views, ranging from 4.5-50.0%, compared with 45.5-81.5% on complete-overlap examples. Moreover, cross-view consistency remains around 50% across models, including recent proprietary VLMs, despite substantially stronger performance on spatial reasoning. Our results reveal a substantial gap between processing multiple images and integrating evidence across them, highlighting the need for evaluations that explicitly test scene-level multi-view understanding. Code and data are available at https://github.com/swathisuhas/OneScene-Bench.

</details>


### [67] [GradSplat: Gradients Inside the Forward Pass for Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38951)
*Jingong Chen,Linlian Jiang,Guangyuan Li,Xinxin Zuo,Yang Wang,Chulwoo Pack,Kyle Gao,Kwanghee Won*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GradSplat：在前向传播内用可学习梯度步精化自身高斯，无需目标视图即可显著缩小前馈3DGS与逐场景优化之间的精度差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D高斯泼溅（feed-forward 3DGS）虽能从少量视图预测场景，但精度低于针对同一批图像逐场景优化的高斯；作者分析该差距主要来自高斯形状（尺度与旋转），单优化形状即可恢复绝大部分性能。

Method: 提出两项技术：（1）对数各向异性校正（log-anisotropy correction），在对数空间、保持体积不变的前提下改变每个高斯的三轴比率；（2）前向梯度精化（in-forward gradient refinement），将高斯渲染到输入视图，计算光度损失梯度，并用可学习循环更新模块先更新不透明度与颜色、再更新形状。整体构成前馈模型 GradSplat，推理时无需目标视图。

Result: 在RealEstate10K两个完整六视图协议上，九种方法中PSNR与SSIM最优，且在6至42个输入视图下保持精度；无需微调即在DL3DV上最准，在ScanNet++上取得最佳PSNR与SSIM。

Conclusion: 通过在推理前向过程中对自产高斯做学习式梯度精化，可有效消除形状维度的前馈—优化差距，并展现良好的视图数量鲁棒性与跨数据集泛化性；代码将于 https://github.com/Jingong-Chen/GradSplat/ 发布。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting predicts a scene from a few images, but our predictor is less accurate than its own Gaussians optimized per scene on those images. This gap lies mostly in the shape of the Gaussians, their scale and rotation, since optimizing the shape alone recovers almost all of it and opacity and color recover less. Gradient steps on the input views correct this shape, and the model holds these views at inference.
